Synthetic data augmentation offers a principled solution, but conventional generative models under-represent distributional tails and give no guarantee against operationally infeasible instances, such as a short air time paired with a long flight distance. No existing approach addresses both limitations for mixed-type tabular records. We propose TailBooster, a dual-layer generative framework combining generative modelling with two anomaly detection layers. A statistical layer extracts extremes via the interquartile range, supplying tail-concentrated training signal to dedicated generative models, here a Tabular Variational Autoencoder. A deep learning layer then applies autoencoder-based cleaning, discarding synthetic records that violate the operational envelope learned from historical data. The framework was evaluated on US flight records across five dimensions: diversity, statistical similarity, fidelity, operational validity, and utility, the latter two being the primary improvement targets. Data-driven cleaning markedly improved operational validity, while targeted augmentation enhanced utility for extreme-event prediction. Across six regression algorithms, training on the framework's records reduced Mean Absolute Error by 47-49% on extreme air time and 29-57% on extreme arrival delay prediction relative to conventional synthetic data, with comparable gains when real records were enriched with synthetic extremes. Being fully data-driven and model-agnostic, TailBooster extends to domains where extreme-event prediction is critical and domain-specific rules are unavailable.

</details>


### [230] [LoongReflect: Boosting Long-Horizon Reflection in Search Agents via Global Perspective Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11967)
*Zhixin Zhang,Xinke Jiang,Zhibang Yang,Weixuan Xu,Guohong Qiu,Xu Chu,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoongReflect 是一个训练框架，通过将反思建模为记忆控制策略，结合快速通道（特权教师蒸馏）和慢速通道（GRPO 结果优化）的互补信号，解决长程推理中反思学习的局部-全局不匹配问题，在多跳 RAG 和数学推理任务上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在长程推理中需要反思能力来评估轨迹进展、识别缺失证据和不可靠中间状态，但反思是局部执行的，其效用只能通过最终轨迹结果判断，导致局部-全局不匹配，使得基于结果的强化学习只能提供局部、稀疏且延迟的监督信号。

Method: LoongReflect 将反思形式化为一个记忆控制策略，智能体在可逆轨迹树上通过显式的反思和回溯操作运行。反思将验证的事实、缺失证据和分支风险整合到工作记忆中；回溯移除不可靠分支并保留简洁的纠正教训。学习时采用两种互补信号：快速通道从特权教师蒸馏全局信息，仅监督反思和回溯 token；慢速通道使用基于结果的 GRPO 优化完整轨迹，使局部控制决策与最终任务成功对齐。

Result: 在多跳检索增强生成和数学推理基准上的实验表明，LoongReflect 一致优于仅使用结果的强化学习和自蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: LoongReflect 通过结合全局蒸馏和局部结果优化，有效解决了反思学习中的局部-全局不匹配问题，提升了模型在多步推理中的反思和回溯能力。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on long-horizon reasoning to solve complex tasks involving planning, tool use, and memory. A critical capability in such settings is reflection: assessing trajectory progress, identifying missing evidence and unreliable intermediate states, and deciding whether to continue, revise, or abandon the current branch. Learning effective reflection, however, is challenging because reflection is performed locally within the current branch, whereas its utility can only be determined by its contribution to the final trajectory outcome. This local-global mismatch makes outcome-based reinforcement learning provide only local, sparse and delayed supervision for reflective decisions. To solve these, we propose LoongReflect, a training framework that formulates reflection as a memory-control policy. The agent operates over a reversible trajectory tree using explicit reflect and backtrack actions. Reflection consolidates verified facts, missing evidence, and branch-specific risks into working memory, while backtracking removes an unreliable branch from the active context and preserves a concise corrective lesson. To learn this policy, LoongReflect combines two complementary signals through a look-ahead, extragradient-style coordination mechanism. A fast channel distills globally informed reflective behavior from a privileged teacher, with supervision restricted to reflection and backtracking tokens. A slow channel optimizes complete trajectories using outcome-based GRPO, aligning local control decisions with final task success. Experiments on multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation and mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over outcome-only reinforcement learning and self-distillation baselines.

</details>


### [231] [TESLA: Taylor Expansion of Sinusoidal Learnable Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11970)
*Daehwa Ko,Jaehyeon Kim,Seunghyun Ham,Jay Hoon Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TESLA激活函数，可学习地组合正弦和余弦项，显式控制多项式阶数并选择性放大高频成分。在32位奇偶校验任务中仅用10万样本（占输入空间0.002%）实现强泛化，对30%标签噪声保持鲁棒；在Forrelation和ImageNet-100上也取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经网络因线性不可分和缺乏全局交互难以解决奇偶校验问题，且常用激活函数难以捕捉高频结构。

Method: 定义TESLA激活函数为可学习的正弦和余弦项线性组合，可显式控制多项式阶数并选择性放大高阶分量；理论上通过约束系数获得Lipschitz/Rademacher复杂度界，并引导训练动态强调高频结构。

Result: 在n=32奇偶校验任务上，TESLA用10万样本（输入空间0.002%）实现强泛化，在30%标签噪声下仍保持高准确率；在Forrelation任务上优于SIREN、SNAKE、傅里叶特征嵌入等基线；在ImageNet-100上取得可比性能。

Conclusion: 激活层级别的阶数控制可迁移到通用视觉任务，为设计显式高频偏好激活函数提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The parity problem--deciding whether the number of ones in a binary vector is odd or even--remains challenging for standard neural networks due to linear inseparability and the need for global interactions. We propose TESLA, an activation defined as a learnable combination of sine and cosine terms, enabling explicit control over polynomial degree and selective amplification of high-order components. Theoretically, we show that constraining TESLA's coefficients yields Lipschitz/Rademacher complexity bounds and shapes the training dynamics to emphasize higher-frequency structure. Empirically, on parity with input length n = 32, TESLA attains strong generalization with 100K training samples (approximately 0.002% of the 2^32 input space) and remains robust under heavy corruption, retaining high accuracy with up to 30% label noise. We also compare against periodic and frequency-based baselines (SIREN, SNAKE, and Fourier feature embeddings) on parity and Forrelation. Beyond synthetic structure, TESLA delivers comparable performance on ImageNet-100, indicating that activation-level degree control transfers to more general vision workloads. Code: https://github.com/KAU-QuantumAILab/TESLA

</details>


### [232] [Remote Sensing and Machine Learning-Based Analysis of Land Use and Vegetation Change in Dhaka District, Bangladesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12001)
*Muhammad Masud Tarek,Md. Alamgir Hossain,Md. Samiul Islam,Muntasir Hasan Kanchan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用遥感数据与机器学习技术，分析了2019至2024年间孟加拉国达卡县的土地覆盖与植被动态变化，揭示了城市扩张导致的植被和水体显著减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速城市化导致达卡县土地利用和环境状况发生重大变化，需要系统监测以支持城市规划与生态可持续性。

Method: 采用Sentinel-2 MSI和Landsat 8高分辨率卫星影像，计算NDVI、NDBI、NDWI等光谱指数，并在Google Earth Engine中使用决策树、K近邻和随机森林监督机器学习分类器，结合标记地理训练点进行分类，通过混淆矩阵和Kappa统计量进行精度评估。

Result: 城市建成区面积增加了59.5%，植被（-8.46%）和水体（-7.77%）显著减少；植被和水域向城市基础设施的转化是主导趋势；随机森林分类器表现最优。

Conclusion: 无序城市扩张加剧了环境压力，遥感与机器学习工具能提供及时、可操作的数据，支持可持续城市发展、土地利用监管和生态系统保护政策。

Abstract: Rapid urbanization in Dhaka District, Bangladesh has triggered substantial alterations in land use and environmental conditions, necessitating systematic monitoring for informed urban planning and ecological sustainability. This study employs remote sensing data and machine learning techniques to analyze spatiotemporal changes in land cover and vegetation dynamics between 2019 and 2024. High-resolution satellite imagery from Sentinel-2 MSI and Landsat 8 was utilized to classify land cover types and compute spectral indices including the Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI), Normalized Difference Built-up Index (NDBI), and Normalized Difference Water Index (NDWI). A supervised machine learning approach incorporating Decision Tree, K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN), and Random Forest classifiers was applied using labeled geospatial training points within Google Earth Engine. Accuracy assessments were conducted using confusion matrices and kappa statistics. Results indicate a 59.5% increase in urban built-up areas and a significant decline in vegetation (-8.46%) and water bodies (-7.77%) over the five-year period. Land conversion from vegetated and aquatic areas to urban infrastructure was identified as a dominant trend. Among the models, Random Forest demonstrated the highest classification accuracy. These findings underscore the growing environmental pressures driven by unregulated urban expansion in Dhaka. The study highlights the potential of remote sensing and machine learning tools in providing timely, actionable data to support sustainable urban development, land-use regulation, and ecosystem conservation policies.

</details>


### [233] [Dual-Model Sentiment Analysis of Consumer Reviews in the Retail Coffee Sector Using Machine Learning and Deep Learning Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12007)
*Muntasir Hasan Kanchan,Md. Alamgir Hossain,Md. Samiul Islam,Muhammad Masud Tarek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对 Starbucks 消费者评论进行的情感分析比较研究，使用经典机器学习（逻辑回归、SVM、决策树、随机森林、朴素贝叶斯）和深度学习（LSTM、RNN、双向 LSTM、GRU、CNN）方法，基于 ConsumerAffairs 的 700 多条评论数据。SVM 在机器学习模型中准确率最高（91.0%），双向 LSTM 在深度学习模型中表现最佳。研究还发现类别不平衡对积极情感召回率产生了负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费者评论在塑造品牌认知和商业策略中扮演重要角色，尤其在零售咖啡等服务驱动型行业。本研究旨在构建一个比较性情感分析框架，评估不同机器学习与深度学习方法在真实消费者评论情感分析中的表现。

Method: 从 ConsumerAffairs 收集了超过 700 条 Starbucks 评论，经过预处理和探索性数据分析识别时间和地理模式。将星级评分二值化（4–5 为正面，1–3 为负面）生成情感标签。采用五种机器学习分类器（逻辑回归、SVM、决策树、随机森林、朴素贝叶斯）和五种深度学习模型（LSTM、RNN、双向 LSTM、GRU、CNN），使用准确率、精确率、召回率和 F1 分数评估性能。

Result: SVM 在机器学习模型中达到最高准确率 91.0%；双向 LSTM 在深度学习模型中表现最强，且对未见数据具有良好的泛化能力。类别不平衡问题导致多数模型对正面情感的召回率较低。

Conclusion: 该研究为零售咖啡行业的消费者情感分析提供了机器学习与深度学习方法的比较评估，强调了合理的模型选择与预处理对于客户体验分析的重要性。

Abstract: Consumer reviews play an important role in shaping brand perception and business strategies, particularly in service-driven industries such as retail coffee. This study presents a comparative sentiment analysis framework for Starbucks customer reviews using classical machine learning and deep learning approaches. The dataset, collected from ConsumerAffairs, contains more than 700 reviews and was analyzed through preprocessing and exploratory data analysis to identify temporal and geographic patterns. Sentiment labels were generated by binarizing star ratings, with ratings of 4 and 5 classified as positive and ratings of 1 to 3 as negative. The resulting dataset was substantially imbalanced toward negative sentiment. Five machine learning classifiers, including Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machine (SVM), Decision Tree, Random Forest, and Naive Bayes, were evaluated alongside five deep learning models: LSTM, RNN, Bidirectional LSTM, GRU, and CNN. Model performance was assessed using accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score. SVM achieved the highest accuracy among the machine learning models at 91.0 percent, while Bidirectional LSTM showed the strongest performance among the deep learning models and demonstrated good generalization on unseen data. The findings also show that class imbalance negatively affected positive sentiment recall across several models. Overall, this study provides a comparative evaluation of machine learning and deep learning approaches for real-world consumer sentiment analysis and highlights the importance of appropriate model selection and preprocessing for customer experience analytics in the retail coffee sector.

</details>


### [234] [Reducing Symmetry Increase in Equivariant Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12010)
*Ning Lin,Jiacheng Cen,Anyi Li,Wenbing Huang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了等变神经网络中对称性增加的现象，证明了对称性增加的下确界由特征空间结构决定，提出了可计算的下确界求解算法和特征设计准则，并在理论、可视化及实验（合成数据集和QM9）上验证了所提框架能有效降低有害的对称性增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变神经网络在处理对称输入时会出现表达力下降的问题，即输出表示的对称性超出输入本身的对称性（对称性增加）。已有研究仅记录了个别案例，缺乏对成因的严格理解和通用的降低策略。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供对称性增加的精确刻画并建立减少该现象的通用框架。

Method: (i) 对于任意特征空间和输入对称群，证明增加的对称性存在一个由特征空间结构决定的下确界；(ii) 基于此下确界，开发可计算的求解算法，并提出实际的特征设计指南以避免有害的对称性增加；(iii) 在标准正则性假设下，证明该指南对大多数等变映射能有效降低对称性增加。

Result: 理论证明了对称性增加的下确界存在且可由特征空间结构确定；开发了可计算下确界的算法；提出特征设计指南；在合成数据集和QM9数据集上的实验及可视化验证了理论预测，表明所提指南能有效降低对称性增加。

Conclusion: 本文为等变神经网络中对称性增加现象提供了严格的数学刻画和可操作的降低策略，为设计更具表达力的等变网络提供了理论指导，并在实验中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Equivariant Neural Networks (ENNs) have empowered numerous applications in scientific fields. Despite their remarkable capacity for representing geometric structures, ENNs suffer from degraded expressivity when processing symmetric inputs: the output representations are invariant to transformations that extend beyond the input's symmetries. The mathematical essence of this phenomenon is that a symmetric input, after being processed by an equivariant map, experiences an increase in symmetry. While prior research has documented symmetry increase in specific cases, a rigorous understanding of its underlying causes and general reduction strategies remains lacking. In this paper, we provide a detailed and in-depth characterization of symmetry increase together with a principled framework for its reduction: (i) For any given feature space and input symmetry group, we prove that the increased symmetry admits an infimum determined by the structure of the feature space; (ii) Building on this foundation, we develop a computable algorithm to derive this infimum, and propose practical guidelines for feature design to prevent harmful symmetry increases. (iii) Under standard regularity assumptions, we demonstrate that for most equivariant maps, our guidelines effectively reduce symmetry increase. To complement our theoretical findings, we provide visualizations and experiments on both synthetic datasets and the real-world QM9 dataset. The results validate our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [235] [AI4AI at Test-Time: Strong-to-Weak Capability Transfer via Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12307)
*Cheng Qian,Wenting Zhao,Liangwei Yang,Heng Wang,Jielin Qiu,Heng Ji,Silvio Savarese,Huan Wang,Shelby Heinecke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“强到弱脚手架”方法，在测试时由强模型为弱模型构建推理支架以实现能力迁移，无需参数更新。在心智理论任务上，该方法将弱模型性能从0.49提升至0.91，表明测试时支架设计是传统训练时蒸馏的重要替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识蒸馏依赖训练时参数更新来迁移能力。本文旨在探索是否可以在测试时（而非训练时）完成能力迁移，从而无需重新训练弱模型。

Method: 提出“强到弱脚手架”范式：一个更强的“构建者”模型利用5%的验证数据迭代优化推理支架（harness），该支架在测试时引导弱“目标”模型解题。目标模型参数完全冻结。实验在四个代表性心智理论（Theory-of-Mind）基准上进行。

Result: 目标模型平均性能近乎翻倍（从0.49到0.91）。增益主要源于将不稳定推理卸载至确定性代码、基准特定路由和严格格式检查，而非更长的推理链或更广采样。构建者自身能力直接影响支架质量，且弱模型收益最大。

Conclusion: 测试时推理支架设计是训练时蒸馏的重要补充，使得强模型无需重新训练即可将结构化认知能力迁移给弱模型。

Abstract: Recent work on distillation transfers the capabilities of large models to smaller ones often by updating the latter's parameters, through teacher forcing, on-policy distillation, and related training-time methods. In this paper, we ask whether such transfer can instead occur at test time. We study strong-to-weak scaffolding: whether a stronger builder model can construct inference-time harnesses that help a weaker target model solve tasks more reliably without any parameter updates. Using four representative Theory-of-Mind benchmarks, each builder model uses 5% of the data as a validation set to iteratively refine its harness over multiple rounds, after which the finalized harness is evaluated on the full test set. Empirically, this form of test-time capability transfer is highly effective, nearly doubling average target-model performance from 0.49 to 0.91. Our analysis shows that the gains come primarily from offloading unstable model reasoning into deterministic code, benchmark-specific routing, and strict answer-format enforcement, rather than from encouraging the target model to reason more extensively or sample more broadly. We further find that builder-model reasoning effort improves harness quality monotonically, platform effects are modest relative to the builder model's own capability, and weaker target models receive the largest gains. These results suggest that inference-time harness design is an important complement to conventional training-time distillation, enabling strong models to transfer cognitive structure to weaker models without retraining.

</details>


### [236] [SoftWater: Class-Aware Rate Allocation for Softmax Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12026)
*Joao V. Cavalcanti,Ashia C. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对LLM中softmax输出层（head）的量化方法SoftWater，将其建模为KL散度下的率失真问题，利用类感知几何和可分离近似实现高效量化。在1B-32B模型上相比WaterSIC在59/60测试点更优，2比特下head引入的KL降低6.5×-8.3×，在Llama-3.2-1B上2比特head可移除45-60%存储字节仅增加2.9-3.7%困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后训练量化流程通常将softmax输出层保留在高精度（如fp16），但在小LLM中head参数占比达15-30%，导致名义上的“2-bit”模型实际平均比特数远高于2-bit。需要对head进行有效量化以真正降低模型存储和部署成本。

Method: 将softmax层量化建模为KL散度下的率失真问题；通过二阶分析揭示类感知几何——量化误差由特征协方差和类特定softmax曲率联合加权；提出可分离近似，将Kn×Kn Cholesky分解替换为每个类重新缩放的一次n×n分解，使格码可通过连续干扰消除编码，所有统计量仅需一次前向传递。最终方法SoftWater对频繁低方差的类分配细量化网格，对稀有类分配粗网格。

Result: 在5个1B-32B模型上，SoftWater在59/60个测试点上优于WaterSIC（基于线性层WMSE但非输出KL的最优量化器），在2比特下将head引入的KL降低6.5×-8.3×。在Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct上，2-bit head减少45-60%存储字节，困惑度仅增加2.9-3.7%。在tied模型上4-bit head接近无损，2-bit head困惑度增加低于4%。

Conclusion: SoftWater使得LLM中softmax输出层的量化变得实用，大幅降低存储开销且性能损失可控。校准数据与部署域匹配可获得最低KL。该方法使小型LLM的head量化达到实际可用水平。

Abstract: Post-training quantization pipelines routinely leave the softmax output layer in high precision. Yet in small LLMs with modern vocabularies, the head holds 15--30\% of all parameters, so a nominal ``2-bit'' model with an fp16 head can store several times as many bits per weight. We pose softmax-layer quantization as a rate-distortion problem under the KL divergence between the original and quantized output distributions. A second-order analysis reveals a class-aware geometry: quantization error is weighted jointly by feature covariance and class-specific softmax curvature. A separability approximation replaces the $Kn\times Kn$ Cholesky with one $n\times n$ factorization rescaled per class, making the lattice encodable by successive interference cancellation, with both statistics from a single forward pass. The resulting method, SoftWater, gives fine grids to frequent, low-variance classes and coarse grids to rare ones, a large gap under Zipfian token distributions. Across five models from 1B to 32B, SoftWater outperforms the released WaterSIC quantizer (near-optimal under linear-layer WMSE but not output KL) at matched head rates on 59 of 60 test points, using none of that pipeline's refinements and cutting head-induced KL by $6.5\times$--$8.3\times$ at 2 bits. On Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct with quantized bodies, a 2-bit head removes 45--60\% of stored bytes for a $2.9$--$3.7\%$ perplexity increase. Because the class-side statistic comes from calibration data, matching calibration to the deployment domain gives the lowest KL on that domain throughout. On a tied model, a 4-bit head is near-lossless and a 2-bit head costs under 4\% perplexity, making head quantization of such models practical.

</details>


### [237] [Uncertainty-Aware Probabilistic Constrained Clustering from Entangled Pairwise Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12027)
*Shaojie Zhang,Ke Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了不确定性感知的概率约束聚类（UPCC）形式化，并引入ProbPair角度成对目标和ECI-PP（估计器-校正器-积分器）框架，通过信念估计、校正和可靠性感知集成来改进不完美监督，在概率监督设置下优于现有深度约束聚类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度约束聚类（DCC）方法主要处理硬约束，对软标签仅进行数值处理而非语义理解，而现实中的成对监督通常是实值的，包含内在模糊性、专家判断和随机噪声。本文旨在形式化这种不确定环境下的概率约束聚类问题。

Method: 形式化不确定性感知概率约束聚类（UPCC），定义异质观测过程中的偶然目标并分析条件可识别性；提出ProbPair角度成对目标用于概率关系；构建ECI-PP框架，包含信念估计、校正和可靠性感知集成三个环节，逐步完善不完美的监督信息。

Result: 在多种概率监督设置下，基于多个基准数据集的实验表明，ECI-PP方法优于当前最先进的深度约束聚类方法，并且在共享默认配置下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的UPCC形式化和ECI-PP框架有效处理了现实中的不确定性成对约束，为概率约束聚类提供了可靠且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Pairwise constrained clustering typically relies on hard must-link/cannot-link labels, whereas realistic pairwise supervision may be real-valued and entangle intrinsic ambiguity, expert judgment, and stochastic corruption. Existing deep constrained clustering (DCC) methods mainly target hard, expert-agnostic constraints, treating soft labels mostly numerically rather than semantically. We formalize this setting as uncertainty-aware probabilistic constrained clustering (UPCC), defining a canonical aleatoric target through a heterogeneous observation process and analyzing its conditional identifiability. We introduce ProbPair, an angular pairwise objective for probabilistic relations, and build ECI-PP, an estimator--corrector--integrator framework that refines imperfect supervision via belief estimation, correction, and reliability-aware integration. Across challenging probabilistic supervision settings, experiments on diverse benchmarks show that ECI-PP outperforms state-of-the-art DCC methods and remains robust with a shared default configuration.

</details>


### [238] [Clustered Randomized Smoothing for Stochastic Prediction Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12037)
*Eduardo Figueiredo,Frederik Mathiesen,Julian Schumann,Jens Kober,Arkady Zgonnikov,Luca Laurenti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出聚类α平滑（clustered α-smoothing）框架，通过聚类划分噪声样本、局部应用α平滑并组合为混合分布，解决随机平滑在随机多模态回归中的模式坍塌问题，显著提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机平滑是提升预测鲁棒性的主流技术，但在随机多模态回归场景中，标准随机平滑因模式坍塌（mode collapse）而产生不反映真实分布的均值预测，无法满足安全关键领域的需求。

Method: 提出聚类α平滑框架：(1) 用任意聚类算法划分噪声样本；(2) 在每个聚类内局部应用α平滑；(3) 将各聚类预测组合为混合分布。将平滑分布解释为多个α平滑器的混合，推导出平滑预测落在对应不同模态的紧致区域并集上的概率下界。

Result: 在驾驶模拟器的随机轨迹预测中，相比α平滑，本方法使Wasserstein距离平均降低27%；在四旋翼飞行器控制任务中，相比最先进的随机平滑方法，碰撞率降低81%。

Conclusion: 聚类α平滑框架有效克服了随机平滑在多模态回归中的模式坍塌问题，在保证鲁棒性的同时保留了输出分布的多样性，显著提升了安全关键应用中的性能。

Abstract: Modern stochastic predictors can model rich, multi-modal outcome distributions. However, this expressive power comes with challenges in ensuring robust predictions $-$ a critical requirement in safety-critical domains. Randomized smoothing is a leading technique for improving robustness, particularly against adversarial perturbations. Yet, in stochastic multi-modal regression settings, randomized smoothing often fails due to mode collapse, yielding averaged predictions that do not reflect the underlying distribution. To address this limitation, we propose clustered $α$-smoothing, a framework that (1) partitions noisy samples using an arbitrary clustering algorithm, (2) applies $α$-smoothing locally within each cluster, and (3) combines the resulting predictions into a mixture distribution. By interpreting the smoothing distribution as a mixture of $α$-smoothers, we derive a lower bound on the probability that the smoothed prediction lies within a union of compact regions corresponding to distinct modes. We empirically evaluate our framework on two benchmarks, demonstrating substantial improvements over state-of-the-art methods. In stochastic trajectory prediction on a driving simulator dataset, our approach achieves, on average, a $27\%$ lower Wasserstein distance to the ground-truth distribution compared to $α$-smoothing. In quadrotor control, where modes correspond to distinct feasible paths to a target, our method reduces the collision rate by $81\%$ relative to the state-of-the-art randomized smoothing.

</details>


### [239] [Towards Truly Unsupervised Evaluation of Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12057)
*Hafiz Saud Arshad,Muhammad Rajabinasab,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批判性地讨论了现有无监督特征选择评估方法的缺陷，指出这些方法并非真正的无监督评估，而是基于无监督下游任务的监督评估。作者提出了一种全新的、真正无监督的评估框架，利用主成分分析（PCA）和最优传输（Optimal Transport）来度量特征选择算法的质量，无需任何标签信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于无监督特征选择算法评估的主流方法存在关键设计缺陷，其所谓“无监督”性质名不副实——实际上是在无监督下游任务下进行的监督评估。因此，需要一种真正无需标签信息的无监督评估框架。

Method: 提出一个全新的真正无监督评估框架，该框架利用无监督主成分分析（PCA）和最优传输（Optimal Transport）技术，在不依赖任何标签信息的情况下度量特征选择方法的质量。

Result: 通过理论分析和批判性讨论，揭示了现有无监督评估方法并非真正无监督的本质；所提出的基于PCA和最优传输的框架能够在完全无监督的条件下有效评估特征选择算法。

Conclusion: 现有无监督特征选择评估方法存在根本性设计缺陷，本质上是监督评估；本文提出的PCA与最优传输相结合的评估框架实现了真正意义上的无监督评估，为特征选择算法的评估提供了更可靠的基础。

Abstract: Feature selection is one of the most important and fundamental tasks in data mining, tackled by a family of methods with an established set of evaluation techniques to measure the quality of a specific method. Most of the methods commonly used for the unsupervised evaluation of feature selection algorithms suffer from critical design flaws which question their unsupervised nature. In this paper, we provide a critical discussion on the established allegedly unsupervised evaluation techniques, and shed light on the reasons why they are not truly unsupervised but, at best, supervised evaluation under an unsupervised downstream task. We also propose a novel, truly unsupervised evaluation framework to measure the quality of the feature selection algorithms without any form of information about the labels. The proposed framework utilizes unsupervised Principal Component Analysis, and optimal transport to measure the quality of the feature selection methods in a truly unsupervised manner.

</details>


### [240] [Faithful, Sufficient and Understandable: Rethinking Graph Counterfactual Explanations via Discrete Diffusion Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12083)
*David Bechtoldt,Sidney Bender*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于离散去噪扩散模型的图反事实解释方法GDCE-I，通过在全部编辑空间内进行分布感知的编辑，同时保持数据流形，并建立统一的评估框架，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN具有强大预测能力但缺乏内在解释性，限制了在高风险场景中的应用。现有反事实解释器要么编辑不在数据流形上，要么搜索未覆盖全部编辑空间，无法同时满足两者。

Method: 提出GDCE-I：采用离散去噪扩散模型，结合新颖的离散反演方案，实现分布感知的编辑，能够利用整个域编辑空间。同时推导了一个解释期望框架，并在统一协议下评估所有方法。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，GDCE-I在所定义的框架上大幅优于现有方法。在分子领域，定性展示了GDCE-I能够获得可解释的分布内解决方案。

Conclusion: GDCE-I同时保持了编辑在数据流形上和搜索覆盖全部编辑空间，克服了现有方法的局限，提供了更优的反事实解释。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) achieve strong predictive performance on graph-structured data across domains such as chemistry, biology, and network analysis, yet they provide no intrinsic explanation of their predictions. This limits their adoption in high-stakes and safety-critical settings. Counterfactual explanations address this by revealing the minimal structural modifications that would change a model's prediction. On graphs, however, such a modification is hard to produce. The search space is discrete and combinatorial, and a valid answer must respect categorical node and edge types together with domain rules such as chemical valency in the case of molecular graphs. Existing explainers give up one of two things. Either edits are not held on the data manifold, or the search does not span the full edit space. We propose Graph Diffusion Counterfactual Explanation via Inversion (GDCE-I), which gives up neither. A discrete denoising diffusion model with a novel discrete inversion scheme enables distribution-aware edits leveraging the whole domain edit space. We further address the incomplete and inconsistent evaluation of graph counterfactuals by deriving a framework of explanation desiderata and applying it to every method under one shared protocol. Across four benchmarks, GDCE-I outperforms related work by a large margin on the defined framework. For the molecular domain, we further qualitatively show that GDCE-I attains interpretable in-distribution solutions.

</details>


### [241] [NAE: Normalizing AutoEncoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12084)
*Muhammad Abdur Rafae,Niels Landwehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前流自编码器（flow autoencoders）中使用的损失函数存在次优性，提出了一种名为归一化自编码器（NAE）的模型，通过引入条件损失来对齐代理损失梯度与重构损失梯度，在分子生成、表格数据和图像基准上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流自编码器（包括全维和瓶颈设置）在训练中使用的损失函数是次优的，因为编码器和解码器代理必须与重构损失对齐才能获得更好的效果，而当前方法未能做到这一点。

Method: 提出归一化自编码器（NAE），采用一种新颖的条件损失，将代理损失梯度与重构损失梯度对齐，直接改进现有标准方法。

Result: 在分子生成、表格数据和图像基准等广泛实验中，NAE实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 损失对齐在流自编码器中至关重要，NAE作为一个强大的生成框架被确立；同时证明现有方法使用的损失是次优的，且编码器和解码器必须与重构损失对齐优化。

Abstract: We consider the setting of Normalizing flows with approximate inverses, an established paradigm spanning both full-dimensional ($d=D$) and bottleneck ($d<D$) settings, and group these models under the term flow autoencoders. We present a theoretical investigation into their training dynamics and prove that the proposed loss used by existing approaches is suboptimal; specifically, both encoder and decoder surrogates must be optimized in alignment with reconstruction loss. Guided by these insights, we propose Normalizing Autoencoder (NAE), which employs a novel conditional loss that aligns the surrogate loss gradient with that of reconstruction loss, directly improving upon the current standard. Extensive experiments across molecule generation, tabular data, and image benchmarks demonstrate that NAE achieves state of the art performance. Our work highlights the importance of loss alignment in flow autoencoders and establishes NAE as a powerful generative framework.

</details>


### [242] [Task- and dataset-specific information in protein language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12090)
*Roman Joeres,Ilya Senatorov,Olga V. Kalinina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了13个蛋白质语言模型（PLMs）在15个下游任务中的中间层嵌入信息量，发现最后一层嵌入并非最优，且不同任务类型对应不同层级的嵌入更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管PLMs广泛使用最后一层嵌入用于下游任务，但其内部行为尚不明确，需要系统探究各层嵌入的信息含量及与任务的相关性。

Method: 对13个PLMs、15个下游任务、11个数据集，通过探针模型评估每一层嵌入的性能，并计算潜在空间特征以估计信息含量。

Result: 最后一层嵌入很少取得最佳下游性能；与预训练目标相似的任务在深层表现更好；整体蛋白质任务中数据集类型决定最佳层（DMS数据适合浅层，天然多样性蛋白适合深层）；人工蛋白质任务性能显著下降。

Conclusion: PLMs的嵌入选择应因任务而异，需考虑任务目标、数据集性质和蛋白质来源，而非默认使用最后一层。

Abstract: Protein language models (PLMs) have transferred the latest advances from natural language processing to computational biology. These models, trained on large corpora of protein sequence data, are widely used to translate amino acid sequences into latent-space embeddings, ready for use in diverse downstream tasks (DTs). By a common consensus, embeddings from the model's last layer are used, and the model's internal behavior remains poorly understood. We analyzed 13 PLMs across 15 DTs from 11 datasets to investigate the informativeness of embeddings created in intermediate PLM layers. We trained probe models on embeddings from each layer, compared their performance, and computed characteristics of the latent spaces they span to estimate the information they contain, and found that the last layers of PLMs rarely contained embeddings that led to the best results on downstream tasks. Furthermore, we identified a connection between DTs and the distribution across PLMs' layers of the relevant information to predict that task. For example, similarity between the pre-training objective and the objective of predicting properties of individual residues leads to a steady increase in understanding of such tasks across the layers of PLMs. On the other hand, for whole-protein tasks, we observe that the dataset, rather than the task itself, defines PLMs' ability to perform well on a DT. Embeddings from shallow layers of PLMs perform better for datasets that contain deep mutational scan (DMS) data, while datasets containing diverse natural proteins find most useful embeddings in the models' deeper layers. Additionally, we discover that the performance of PLMs drops significantly when tasks are introduced for artificial proteins.

</details>


### [243] [Confidence Calibration of Deep Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12100)
*Coby Penso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对高可靠性应用场景中深度学习的置信度校准问题，提出了在标签噪声、领域偏移和隐私保护约束下的校准方法，包括噪声感知的置信度重建与保形预测、无监督领域自适应的无标签校准，以及本地差分隐私保形预测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度校准方法通常假设存在干净的验证数据，但在实际部署中标签噪声和领域偏移普遍存在，导致标准方法产生误导性置信度估计。同时，隐私保护要求进一步增加了校准难度。因此需要开发能在这些非理想条件下依然可靠工作的校准方法。

Method: 1) 针对标签噪声：利用估计的噪声模型重建无噪声置信度估计，并扩展至保形预测（CP），估计干净的符合分数。2) 针对无监督领域自适应：从源域性能与领域差异估计目标域准确率，实现无目标标签的校准。3) 针对隐私保护：提出本地差分隐私保形预测框架，在保证隐私的同时提供有效的不确定性量化。

Result: 所提出的方法在标签噪声、领域偏移和隐私保护约束下均能实现可靠的置信度校准，填补了校准理论与安全关键型实际部署之间的鸿沟。

Conclusion: 该论文系统性地解决了实际部署中因数据质量问题（标签噪声、领域偏移）和隐私约束导致的校准失效问题，为高可靠性应用提供了噪声鲁棒、隐私保护的神经网络预测方案，推动了校准理论在实际安全关键场景中的应用。

Abstract: In high-stakes applications, reliable confidence estimates are as important as the predictions themselves. Confidence calibration ensures that predicted probabilities reflect the likelihood of correctness, making it essential for safe deployment of deep learning models. However, existing methods typically assume access to clean validation data, which is often unrealistic due to label noise and domain shifts. This thesis develops methods for improving calibration under these conditions.
  First, we address calibration under label noise. Standard methods can produce misleading confidence estimates when labels are unreliable. We propose a framework that uses an estimated noise model to reconstruct noise-free confidence estimates by modeling the relationship between noisy and clean label distributions. We extend this approach to Conformal Prediction (CP), which provides set-valued predictions with guaranteed coverage. Our noise-aware CP method estimates clean conformity scores despite label noise, enabling reliable uncertainty quantification. Next, we study calibration in unsupervised domain adaptation, where a model trained on a labeled source domain is adapted to an unlabeled target domain. Since labeled target data are unavailable, we estimate target-domain accuracy from source performance and domain discrepancies, enabling calibration without target labels. We also consider privacy-preserving settings in which user labels and model outputs must remain protected. We propose a locally differentially private conformal prediction framework that provides valid uncertainty quantification while maintaining privacy guarantees and balancing privacy, computational feasibility, and prediction reliability.
  Our results bridge calibration theory and practical deployment in safety-critical applications, contributing to reliable, privacy-preserving, and noise-resilient neural network predictions.

</details>


### [244] [Beyond Parameter Space: NTK-Guided Personalized Aggregation for Robust Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12108)
*Mirko Konstantin,Stefan Zachow,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LIGHTYEAR是一个面向异构联邦学习的框架，通过在函数空间（而非参数空间）利用神经正切核（NTK）一致性评分来评估客户端更新的预测行为，结合点对点（P2P）拓扑和正则化聚合规则，为每个客户端个性化选择有益的更新，显著优于集中式FL和现有P2P方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习通常基于参数空间（模型参数或梯度）的相似性来选择客户端更新，但在非IID异构数据下，参数空间相似性无法有效反映预测行为，导致可能聚合与目标域不匹配的更新（由异构数据或故障客户端引起），降低本地模型性能。

Method: 提出LIGHTYEAR框架：1）使用神经正切核（NTK）计算一致性评分，在函数空间表征预测行为；2）采用点对点（P2P）拓扑，客户端直接交换更新并用私有验证数据评估；3）每个客户端基于NTK评分选择对其目标域有益的更新；4）使用正则化聚合规则提高异构性下的稳定性。

Result: 在五个数据集、九个基线方法上，LIGHTYEAR始终优于集中式联邦学习基线以及现有的P2P方法。

Conclusion: 函数空间的更新选择比参数空间更有效，LIGHTYEAR通过NTK协议评分和P2P拓扑实现了对异构联邦学习场景的个性化聚合，提升了模型性能和稳定性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed clients while keeping data local. A central challenge is determining which client updates are beneficial for aggregation with respect to each client's target domain. Existing methods typically address this problem in parameter space by comparing model parameters or gradients. However, parameter-space similarity can be a poor proxy for predictive behavior, especially under heterogeneous, non-IID data. Consequently, updates that are misaligned with a client's target domain, including those caused by heterogeneous data or malfunctioning clients, may degrade local model performance.
  We propose Local Inference Guided Aggregation for Heterogeneous Training Environments to Yield Enhancement Through Agreement and Regularization (LIGHTYEAR), a federated learning framework that performs update selection in function space. LIGHTYEAR uses an NTK-based agreement score to characterize predictive behavior and determine a personalized aggregation set for each client. By relating model parameters to local predictive responses, the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) provides a more expressive criterion for update selection than parameter-space similarity alone.
  Because function-space information is not available before aggregation in conventional centralized FL, LIGHTYEAR uses a peer-to-peer (P2P) topology in which clients exchange updates directly and evaluate incoming models on private validation data. Each client selects only updates that are beneficial for its own target domain and aggregates them using a regularized rule that improves stability under heterogeneity.
  Across five datasets and nine baseline methods, LIGHTYEAR consistently outperforms centralized FL baselines and existing P2P approaches.

</details>


### [245] [Attractor Image-Based Deep Learning of Arterial Pulse Waves for Age Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12117)
*Sara Vardanega,Patrick Segers,Philip Aston,Ernst Rietzschel,Jordi Alastruey,Manasi Nandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用光电容积脉搏波（PPG）和动脉张力计脉冲波时间序列数据，通过对称投影吸引子重构（SPAR）方法将其转换为图像，然后使用卷积神经网络（CNN）对健康成年人的两个相近年龄组（35-40岁与50-55岁）进行分类，在内部和外部测试集上均取得了超过70%的F1分数，证明SPAR图像包含可区分年龄的形态学特征，为未来利用智能穿戴设备进行早期风险检测奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动脉脉搏波形态随年龄演变，可反映心血管系统结构和功能变化，因此血管年龄是心血管健康的重要替代标志物，早衰可提示疾病风险。脉搏波分析有助于在无症状成年人中进行风险分层，但目前缺乏有效的方法从脉冲波时间序列中提取判别性特征用于相近年龄组的分类。

Method: 从PPG和动脉张力计获取脉冲波时间序列数据，采用对称投影吸引子重构（SPAR）方法将时间序列转换为图像；利用这些SPAR图像训练卷积神经网络（CNN），对健康受试者的两个相近年龄组（35-40岁和50-55岁）进行分类。

Result: 模型在内部和外部测试集上均表现出一致的分类性能，PPG和动脉张力计信号的F1分数均超过70%。

Conclusion: SPAR衍生的脉冲波图像即使在年龄相近的健康成年人中也包含可区分的形态学特征；该概念验证为未来利用智能可穿戴设备通过SPAR进行早期风险检测的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Arterial pulse waveform morphology evolves with age, reflecting structural and functional changes in the cardiovascular system. Thus, vascular age is a valuable surrogate marker of cardiovascular health, and premature vascular ageing can indicate increased disease risk. Pulse wave analysis could support risk stratification in otherwise asymptomatic adults. We transformed pulse wave time-series data from photoplethysmography (PPG) and arterial tonometry into images, using the Symmetric Projection Attractor Reconstruction (SPAR) method. These SPAR images were used to train a convolutional neural network to classify healthy subjects into two closely spaced age groups (35-40 and 50-55 years). The model demonstrated consistent classification performance across internal and external test sets, achieving F1 scores above 70% for both PPG and tonometry signals. These results suggest that SPAR-derived pulse wave images contain discriminative morphological features even among healthy adults close in age. This proof-of-concept lays the groundwork for future research into the use of SPAR for early risk detection using smart wearables.

</details>


### [246] [Adversarial Resilience of Poisson-Process Submodular Maximization over Matroids: From Robust Offline Optimization to Full-Bandit Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12134)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对一般拟阵约束下的非负子模最大化问题，在离线算法仅能访问受控价值预言机（即存在有界加性误差 ξ 的任意对抗性预言机）时，证明了“恶意贪婪交换泊松过程”（SGS-Poisson）的对抗鲁棒性定理：该算法无需修改其泊松强度、单元素交换规则或恶意丢弃步骤，仍能保持非单调目标 1/e 和单调目标 1-1/e 的极限近似因子。具体地，在预言机误差有界时，算法以 O~(nk^2 ε^{-2}) 次调用返回期望值至少为 (1/e-ε)OPT-O(kξ) 或 (1-1/e-ε)OPT-O(kξ) 的可行解。由此，通过离线到在线归约，得到全反馈组合多臂赌博机算法，在一般拟阵约束的子模奖励下实现了精确的 1/e 和 1-1/e 极限近似-遗憾因子，遗憾界为 O~(n^{1/5} k^{4/5} T^{4/5})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子模最大化在机器学习、组合优化等领域有广泛应用，但传统算法假设价值预言机精确无误。实际场景中预言机可能受到对抗性噪声或偏差（如人为干扰、测量误差），因此需要设计对预言机误差具有鲁棒性的算法。本文旨在解决一般拟阵约束下非负子模最大化问题在对抗性受控预言机下的鲁棒优化，并为后续在线学习（全反馈组合多臂赌博机）提供基础。

Method: 本文基于“恶意贪婪交换泊松过程”（SGS-Poisson）算法，该算法通过泊松过程控制交换步率、使用单元素交换规则和恶意丢弃步骤来避免局部最优。核心证明是对抗鲁棒性定理：即使预言机被任意对抗性误差 ξ 污染（|f̂(S)-f(S)|≤ξ），算法无需调整任何参数或步骤，其期望近似比仍保持 1/e（非单调）和 1-1/e（单调），仅损失 O(kξ) 的加性项。证明将实际目标函数与估计函数之间的误差传播通过算法流程进行控制。随后，利用该离线鲁棒算法作为子程序，通过离线到在线归约（如探索与利用权衡）得到全反馈 CMAB 算法，达到亚线性遗憾界。

Result: 理论结果分为两部分：(1) 离线鲁棒算法：在受控预言机下，SGS-Poisson 以 O~(nk^2 ε^{-2}) 次预言机调用返回期望值 ≥ (1/e-ε)OPT - O(kξ)（非单调）或 ≥ (1-1/e-ε)OPT - O(kξ)（单调）的可行解。(2) 在线学习：基于离线算法的在线归约得到全反馈 CMAB 算法，对一般拟阵约束子模奖励实现精确的 1/e 和 1-1/e 极限近似-遗憾因子，遗憾界为 O~(n^{1/5} k^{4/5} T^{4/5})。

Conclusion: 本文证明了 SGS-Poisson 算法在对抗性受控预言机下仍保持最优理论近似比，填补了子模最大化鲁棒性研究的空白。其离线到在线归约首次为一般拟阵约束子模奖励的全反馈 CMAB 问题提供了精确的极限近似-遗憾因子匹配。该工作展示了泊松过程与恶意丢弃策略在对抗环境下的稳定性，为后续鲁棒子模优化和在线学习研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study nonnegative submodular maximization subject to a general matroid when the offline algorithm is given an arbitrary controlled value oracle. Our main result is an adversarial resilience theorem for the Spiteful Greedy Swap Poisson Process (SGS-Poisson): without modifying its Poisson intensity, single-element exchange rule, or spiteful drop step, the algorithm retains limiting approximation factors $1/e$ for non-monotone objectives and $1-1/e$ for monotone objectives. More precisely, under every controlled oracle $\widehat f$ satisfying $|\widehat f(S)-f(S)|\le ξ$ for every set $S$, our implementation returns a feasible set with expected value at least $(1/e-\varepsilon)\OPT-O(kξ)$ and $(1-1/e-\varepsilon)\OPT-O(kξ)$, respectively, using $\widetilde O(nk^2\varepsilon^{-2})$ oracle calls. As a consequence, the offline-to-online reduction yields full-bandit CMAB algorithms for general matroid-constrained submodular rewards with exact limiting approximation-regret factors $1/e$ and $1-1/e$ and $\widetilde O(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})$ regret.

</details>


### [247] [HYDRA: Hyperbolic Dynamic Representation Architecture for Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12194)
*Zhao Su,Yuxin Xia,Haoran Li,Jun Shen,Qi Zhu,Qingguo Zhou,Binbin Yong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HYDRA is a hyperbolic extension of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) that reduces parameter redundancy by using a low-rank prototype block to share functional transformations across hidden dimensions, operating in the Poincaré ball. It achieves competitive predictive performance while improving parameter efficiency and interpretability.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KANs assign an independent learnable univariate function to every connection, leading to substantial parameter redundancy that limits scalability and efficiency.

Method: HYDRA maps vector-valued inputs into a bounded hyperbolic latent space (Poincaré ball), performs KAN-style updates in tangent space, and employs a low-rank prototype block to share functional transformations across hidden dimensions, with radius control to prevent boundary saturation.

Result: Across eight benchmark datasets, HYDRA consistently achieves competitive or superior predictive performance while improving parameter efficiency and representation interpretability.

Conclusion: HYDRA provides a parameter-efficient hyperbolic extension of KAN that reduces redundancy, enhances interpretability through structured radial coordinates, and improves training stability via radius control.

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) enhance nonlinear function approximation by replacing scalar weights with learnable univariate functions. However, assigning an independent function to every connection results in substantial parameter redundancy, limiting their scalability and efficiency. To reduce this redundancy, we introduce \textbf{HY}perbolic \textbf{D}ynamic \textbf{R}epresentation \textbf{A}rchitecture (HYDRA), a parameter-efficient hyperbolic extension of KAN that combines spline-based functional learning with representations in the Poincaré ball. HYDRA maps vector-valued inputs into a bounded hyperbolic latent space, performs KAN-style updates in tangent space, and employs a low-rank prototype block to share functional transformations across hidden dimensions. The resulting hyperbolic representations provide a structured radial coordinate for interpretation, while radius control improves training stability by preventing boundary saturation. Extensive experiments across eight benchmark datasets demonstrate that HYDRA consistently achieves competitive or superior predictive performance while improving parameter efficiency and representation interpretability.

</details>


### [248] [ScreenShot: A Foundation Model for Few-Shot Combination Drug Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12219)
*Antoine de Mathelin,Christopher Tosh,Wesley Tansey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ScreenShot是一个层级Transformer模型，预训练于40个药物筛选数据集（涵盖3700种药物和6000个生物样本），能够通过上下文学习从少量功能测量数据中预测新患者对联合治疗的反应，无需分子图谱或微调。在四个测试集上优于所有基线，且其内部表示可用于主动学习策略，以三分之一的预算达到与均匀筛选相同的命中检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合用药可降低耐药风险，但组合筛选空间巨大导致实验昂贵、耗时且技术不可行。现有预测模型通常需要每个样本的分子图谱和每队列训练，在时间和组织样本稀缺时应用受限。

Method: 提出ScreenShot，一个层级Transformer架构，其设计模仿筛选数据的嵌套结构。该模型在40个药物筛选数据集上预训练，覆盖3700种药物和6000个生物样本。通过上下文学习，仅需新患者的少量功能测量作为输入，即可预测其对联合治疗的反应，无需微调也无需分子图谱数据。

Result: 在四个保留数据集上，ScreenShot在预测准确性和选择性有效治疗识别方面均优于所有基线。其内部表示还可用于加权k-means++主动学习策略来指导实验选择，在命中检测上达到与均匀筛选相同的效果，但仅需三分之一的预算。

Conclusion: ScreenShot为联合治疗反应预测提供了一种高效、无需分子图谱的解决方案，并通过主动学习显著降低实验成本，具有重要的临床应用价值。

Abstract: Treating patients with combinations of drugs reduces the risk of resistance to any individual drug. Finding effective combinations is difficult because the large search space makes combinatorial screens prohibitively expensive, time consuming, and often technically infeasible. Predictive models can fill this gap, yet existing methods typically require molecular profiling of each sample and per-cohort training, limiting their applicability when time and tissue are scarce. To address this challenge, we introduce ScreenShot, a hierarchical transformer pretrained on 40 drug screening datasets covering 3,700 drugs and 6,000 biological samples, whose architecture mirrors the nested structure of screening data. Given a few-shot context of observations from a new patient, ScreenShot predicts the response of the sample to combination therapies through in-context learning, operating directly on functional measurements with no fine-tuning and no molecular profiling. On four held-out datasets, ScreenShot outperforms all baselines in both prediction accuracy and identification of selectively effective treatments. ScreenShot's internal representations are directly useful for experimental design: we use them to drive a weighted k-means++ active learning strategy that selects which experiments to run, achieving the same hit detection as uniform screening with a third of the budget. Source code and interactive dashboard: https://github.com/tansey-lab/screenshot.

</details>


### [249] [An Efficient Near-Optimal Algorithm for Adversarial $m$-Set Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12231)
*Francesco Bacchiocchi,Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对m-集合动作的对抗性组合赌博机问题，提出了一种计算高效且多项式时间复杂度的算法，该算法无需枚举指数级大小的动作集，达到了与EXP3-KW算法相同的高概率遗憾界O(√(dT log(K/δ)))，解决了Maiti等人提出的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对抗性组合赌博机中，动作集大小K=C(d,m)可能呈指数级增长，现有算法（如EXP3-KW）虽能达到最优遗憾界，但其直接实现需要指数级空间和计算成本。因此，需要一种计算高效且保持最优遗憾界的算法，以利用动作集的底层结构（由d维物品损失向量决定）来避免显式枚举。

Method: 提出一种计算高效的算法，通过仅用d个参数表示每个采样分布（而非显式维护K个动作的概率），在多项式时间内运行。该算法对抗适应性非预期对手，以概率至少1-δ保证遗憾界O(√(dT log(K/δ)))。

Result: 算法实现了与EXP3-KW相同的遗憾界O(√(dT log(K/δ)))，但计算复杂度从指数级降为多项式级，无需枚举整个动作集。这解决了Maiti等人提出的开放问题。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在对抗性组合赌博机中，可以同时获得计算效率和最优统计性能，为大规模组合决策问题提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We study adversarial combinatorial bandits with $m$-set actions, where at each round the learner selects $m$ out of $d$ items and observes only the aggregate loss of the selected items. The resulting action set contains $K=\binom{d}{m}$ elements and can therefore be exponentially large. Nevertheless, the loss of every action is determined by the same $d$-dimensional vector of item losses. We propose a computationally efficient algorithm that exploits this structure without explicitly enumerating the action set. Against adaptive non-anticipating adversaries, it guarantees, with probability at least $1-δ$, regret against the best fixed action of \[
  R_T =
  O\left(\sqrt{dT\log(K/δ)}\right). \] This matches the high-probability regret bound of the finite-action EXP3-KW algorithm of Zimmert and Lattimore, whose direct implementation may require exponential space. Our algorithm instead represents each sampling distribution with $d$ parameters and runs in polynomial time without enumerating the action set. Thus, it resolves the open problem posed by Maiti et al.

</details>


### [250] [Calibration Bets on the Past: Post-Training Quantization for Financial Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12259)
*Junyi Ye,Ivy Gateri Wanjiku*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了金融预测模型在低精度部署中激活值校准（activation calibration）的影响，发现4位量化时激活校准是预测性能的主要决定因素，采用百分位校准可恢复大部分精度损失，且最优校准范围随市场周期变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融预测模型通常以全精度开发，但生产部署常需低精度推理以降低内存和计算成本。后训练量化（PTQ）无需重新训练即可实现低精度部署，但可靠的激活量化需要校准——即从历史数据估计激活范围并在未来推理中固定使用。这一部署选择对金融预测的重要性尚不清楚，相关研究缺乏系统性。

Method: 对S&P 500横截面波动率预测任务进行系统性研究，涵盖7种代表性神经网络架构、8个滚动测试年份（2018-2025）和560个训练模型。对比不同激活校准方法（绝对最大值校准vs百分位校准）在不同位宽（8位和4位）下的效果，并分析不同市场周期对最优校准范围的影响。

Result: 激活校准在8位量化时影响很小，但在4位量化时成为预测性能的主要决定因素。默认绝对最大值（abs-max）校准下，4位静态量化权重和激活将使受影响架构的全精度平均信息系数（mean information coefficient）降低11-62%。将abs-max替换为百分位校准可恢复受影响最严重的四个架构中53-94%的退化。最优激活范围在不同市场周期中有所变化：窄范围在典型市场条件下分辨率更高，但当测试期市场离散度超过校准历史时，其优势部分丧失。

Conclusion: 激活校正是可靠4位PTQ金融预测中的一等部署决策（first-class deployment decision）。当退化仍然显著时，8位激活或仅权重量化4位提供了更稳健的部署选择。

Abstract: Financial forecasting models are typically developed in full precision, yet production deployment often requires low-precision inference to reduce memory and computational cost. Post-training quantization (PTQ) enables such deployment without retraining. However, reliable activation quantization requires calibration: activation ranges are estimated from historical data before deployment and then remain fixed during future inference. The importance of this deployment choice for financial forecasting remains poorly understood. We present a systematic study of activation calibration for PTQ in cross-sectional volatility forecasting on the S&P 500. Our evaluation covers seven representative neural architectures, eight walk-forward test years (2018-2025), and 560 trained models. We find that activation calibration has little effect at 8 bits but becomes the primary determinant of predictive performance at 4 bits. Under default absolute-maximum (abs-max) calibration, static 4-bit quantization of both weights and activations removes 11-62% of the full-precision mean information coefficient in affected architectures. Replacing abs-max with percentile calibration recovers 53-94% of this degradation in the four most affected architectures. The preferred activation range also varies across market periods. Narrow ranges improve resolution under typical market conditions but lose part of their advantage when test-period market dispersion exceeds the calibration history. These findings show that activation calibration is a first-class deployment decision for reliable 4-bit PTQ in financial forecasting. When substantial degradation remains, 8-bit activations or weight-only 4-bit quantization provide more robust deployment choices.

</details>


### [251] [Earth observation embeddings are effective sub-grid descriptors for probabilistic weather downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12271)
*Pedro Sousa,Will Tebbutt,Sadiq Jaffer,Robin Young,Anil Madhavapeddy,Richard E. Turner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次验证了长期地球观测基础模型嵌入（TESSERA）可用于短期天气预报降尺度，通过提取高分辨率地表特征改进粗网格再分析/预报场的站点级概率预测，在气温和风速上分别提升CRPS技能11.5%和6.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球天气再分析和预报在粗网格上求解大气状态，但站点级应用需要任意位置的高分辨率预测，而近地面条件受未解析的地形和地表特性影响。现有概率降尺度方法依赖手工设计的地形描述符，本文探索地球观测基础模型能否提供可迁移的次网格地表表示用于概率天气降尺度。

Method: 将卷积条件神经过程（convCNP）与TESSERA嵌入结合：在粗分辨率ERA5场（~25 km）输入上，通过压缩10 m分辨率的TESSERA嵌入块学习局部地表描述符，利用条件神经过程进行概率降尺度，预测2 m气温和10 m风速的站点值。

Result: 在五个气候多样区域，嵌入在时空留出站点上提升了点预测和概率技能：气温CRPS技能提升11.5%，风速提升6.2%。地形解释气温的更多次网格结构，而TESSERA为风速提供额外地表信息。改进在输入改为Aurora AI预报模型时保持，且对无区域历史的新站点也有效。

Conclusion: 这是首个证据表明长期地球观测嵌入可支持短期天气降尺度，因为次网格偏差系统性地由持久地表属性结构化。方法可迁移至不同粗网格输入和未观测站点，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Global weather reanalyses and forecasts resolve the evolving atmospheric state on coarse grids, but site-specific applications require predictions at arbitrary locations where near-surface conditions also depend on unresolved terrain and land-surface properties. Existing probabilistic downscalers address this gap using hand-crafted topographic descriptors. We ask instead whether Earth observation foundation models can provide transferable sub-grid surface representations for probabilistic weather downscaling.
  We augment a convolutional conditional neural process that downscales coarse ERA5 reanalysis fields at ~25 km resolution with a learned local surface descriptor, obtained by compressing a patch of TESSERA embeddings at 10 m resolution. Although these embeddings summarise surface conditions over annual timescales, they improve downscaling of instantaneous 2 m temperature and 10 m wind speed by encoding persistent surface properties that capture a location's departure from the coarse-grid atmospheric state. Across five climatically diverse regions, the embedding improves point and probabilistic skill at stations held out in both space and time, overall improving CRPS skill by 11.5% for 2 m temperature and 6.2% for 10 m wind speed. We further analyse how its contribution differs by variable, finding that topography explains more of temperature's sub-grid structure, while TESSERA provides additional surface information for wind speed.
  These improvements persist when the coarse input is changed from ERA5 to forecasts from the Aurora AI forecasting model, and when predicting at newly deployed stations with no regional history. To our knowledge, this is the first evidence that long-timescale Earth-observation embeddings can support short-timescale weather downscaling where sub-grid departures are systematically structured by persistent surface properties.

</details>


### [252] [A Framework for Designing Reward Functions: From Objectives to Features to Human-Aligned Reward Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12302)
*Di Yang Shi,W. Bradley Knox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个形式化流程，使非专家能够实例化和迭代符合人类偏好的奖励函数。该流程通过三个步骤从自然语言任务描述生成线性奖励函数：将任务目标提炼为一组基本目标并推导可测量的结果变量，选择因果代表性结果变量子集作为奖励项，通过偏好 elicitation 拟合权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让非专家也能设计出与人类偏好对齐的奖励函数，无需深厚的技术背景，从而降低奖励设计门槛，使更多人能参与强化学习奖励函数的设计与迭代。

Method: 三步流程：第一步是引导式工作流，从自然语言任务描述中推导出结果变量；第二步将奖励项选择问题归约为因果DAG上的最小成本部分覆盖问题，通过最大流算法在多项式时间内求解；第三步将权重拟合建模为凸可行性问题，通过偏好查询（分离 oracle）迭代缩小可行区域。

Result: 生成的线性奖励函数具有确定性的无冲突可行权重区域，可通过分离 oracle 以 O(n log κ) 次偏好查询将区域缩小到所需容差。

Conclusion: 据我们所知，这是首个保持可行权重区域无冲突的奖励设计方法，支持非专家通过偏好查询迭代细化奖励函数，具有理论保证的高效性。

Abstract: We present a formal process to enable non-experts to instantiate and iterate on human-aligned reward functions, i.e. reward functions that adhere to a given preference ordering over trajectories. Given a task described in natural language, our process produces a linear reward function in three steps: distill the task's objectives into a set of fundamental objectives and derive measurable outcome variables that capture those fundamental objectives, select a causally representative subset of outcome variables as the reward terms, and fit weights to those reward terms via preference elicitation. Our contributions describe the first step and formalize the latter two steps. The first is a guided workflow for deriving outcome variables. The second is a reduction of reward term selection to minimum-cost partial cover on a causal DAG, solved in polynomial time via max-flow. The third is a geometric framing of weight fitting as a convex feasibility problem iteratively narrowed by preference queries, solved by existing separation oracle methods. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first reward-design method that maintains a deterministically conflict-free feasible weight region, narrowed to a desired tolerance via a separation oracle with O(n log κ) preference queries.

</details>


### [253] [Redistribution-based Cost Inference Improves Sparse Safe Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12306)
*Ebenezer Gelo,Geraud Nangue Tasse,Steven James,Benjamin Rosman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离线安全强化学习中监督者仅提供轨迹级停止反馈（二元信号，指示第一次不安全转换）而缺乏逐步骤成本注释的问题，提出基于重分布的成本推断（RCI）框架，通过回报分解将稀疏停止反馈转化为密集逐步骤成本，并利用增强数据集训练约束离线策略。理论上证明回报等价重分布保持可行策略集和CMDP中的最优拉格朗日量，保证转换无损；实验表明在高速公路驾驶和机器人操作任务中，违规率显著低于稀疏和基于分类器的基线，且对异构数据集组成和标签噪声具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际离线安全强化学习中，监督者通常只能提供轨迹级的停止反馈（即首次不安全转换的二元信号），而非密集的逐步骤成本标注。现有方法假设获取密集成本，导致稀疏反馈下的成本推断成为关键挑战。本文旨在解决该时序信用分配问题，实现从稀疏反馈到密集成本的可靠转换，从而支持约束离线策略学习。

Method: 提出基于重分布的成本推断（RCI）框架：首先利用回报分解将轨迹级的稀疏停止反馈转化为密集的逐步骤成本估计；然后在增强数据集上训练约束离线策略。通过理论证明，回报等价的重分布操作在约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）中保持可行策略集和最优拉格朗日量不变，保证转换在理论上无损，并在实践中改善成本评论家学习的条件。

Result: 在高速公路驾驶和机器人操作任务中，RCI方法相比稀疏反馈基线和基于分类器的基线，实现了显著更低的违规率。此外，该方法对异构数据集组成和标签噪声表现出鲁棒性，验证了实用效果。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过回报等价重分布将稀疏停止反馈转换为密集成本信号的理论可行性（保持可行策略集和最优拉格朗日量），同时实验验证了该方法能有效降低违规率并具备鲁棒性，为实际场景中的安全离线强化学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Safe offline RL typically assumes access to dense per-step cost annotations, but in practice supervisors provide only trajectory-level stop-feedback: a binary signal at the first unsafe transition, with no per-step attribution. We frame this as a temporal credit assignment problem and propose the Redistribution-based Cost Inference (RCI) framework, which converts sparse stop-feedback into dense per-step costs via return decomposition, then trains a constrained offline policy on the augmented dataset. We show that return-equivalent redistribution preserves the feasible policy set and the optimal Lagrangian in a CMDP, establishing that the transformation is lossless in theory while yielding better-conditioned cost critic learning in practice. Experiments on highway driving and robotic manipulation demonstrate substantially lower violation rates than sparse and classifier-based baselines, with robustness to heterogeneous dataset compositions and label noise.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [254] [Dynamic Governance of Multi-LLM Agent Systems for Collaborative Conversational Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11207)
*Alexander Liss,Nicholas Desmond,Santiago Gil Gallego*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当两个目标对立的LLM智能体进行多轮交互时，由于缺乏共享目标函数，系统不是产生竞争而是走向崩溃：访客屈服，网站智能体停止调整策略，对话在未达成任何一方目标前终止。本文提出Experience Orchestrator（EO）作为控制理论治理层来替代缺失的目标函数。EO在模拟金融服务环境中运行，通过三种机制（情境赌博机CB、PID控制器、POMDP信念追踪器）管理联合轨迹。在60,000次模拟中，EO将高意向顾问联系率提升32个百分点（78.1% vs 46.1%），其中CB变体选择解释了97%的因子间结果方差——证实是治理策略而非环境初始条件决定了轨迹终点。人物分析揭示了两种不同机制：对于无自然转化倾向的访客，治理层是功能性系统与非功能性系统的区别；对于已接近一致的访客，朴素LLM的共情默认值已基本足够。所有发现均以LLM到LLM模拟为条件，PID控制器尚未针对真实人类不可预测性进行校准，在真实流量上验证EO是下一步关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个具有结构性对立目标的LLM智能体在多次交互时，由于缺乏共享目标函数，系统会陷入崩溃而非产生有效竞争。这种"目标缺失"问题导致访客投降、网站代理停止调整策略，对话在未达成任何一方目标前终止。作者试图通过控制理论治理层来替代缺失的目标函数，使对立智能体的多轮交互能够产生有意义的结果，而不是陷入死锁或崩溃。

Method: 提出Experience Orchestrator（EO）框架，在模拟金融服务环境中运行。EO包含三个核心机制：(1) 情境赌博机（Contextual Bandit, CB）——基于真实网页分析数据校准的内容臂选择；(2) PID控制器——通过动态模式约束强制执行行为一致性；(3) POMDP信念追踪器——维护访客意图的概率模型。通过在60,000次模拟中对比EO与朴素LLM对照组，评估不同治理策略的效果。

Result: EO将高意向顾问联系率提升32个百分点（78.1% vs 46.1%朴素LLM对照组）。CB变体选择解释了97%的因子间结果方差，证实治理策略而非环境初始条件决定轨迹终点。人物层面分析揭示两种机制：对于无自然转化倾向的访客，治理层是系统功能性的决定因素；对于已接近一致的访客，朴素LLM的共情默认值已足够。

Conclusion: 治理层（EO）可以有效替代对立LLM智能体间缺失的共享目标函数，使系统产生有意义的交互结果而非崩溃。但所有发现基于LLM到LLM模拟，PID控制器尚未针对真实人类不可预测性校准，在真实流量上验证EO是下一步关键任务。

Abstract: When two LLM agents with structurally opposed objectives interact across multiple turns, the absence of a shared goal function produces not competition but collapse: the visitor capitulates, the site agent stops varying its approach, and the conversation terminates without achieving either agent's stated objective. This paper asks whether a control-theoretic governance layer can substitute for that missing goal function. The Experience Orchestrator (EO) addresses this in a simulated financial services environment where a site agent guides a visitor toward advisor contact while the visitor maintains psychologically realistic resistance. EO governs the joint trajectory through three mechanisms: a Contextual Bandit (CB) that selects content arms calibrated from real-world web analytics, a PID controller that enforces behavioral consistency via dynamic schema constraints, and a POMDP belief tracker that maintains a probabilistic model of visitor intent. Across 60,000 simulations, EO achieves a +32 percentage point lift in high-intent advisor contact rate (78.1% vs. 46.1% over a naive LLM control), with CB variant selection accounting for 97% of between-factor outcome variance -- confirming that the governance policy, not environmental initial conditions, determines where trajectories end up. Persona-level analysis reveals two distinct regimes: for visitors with no natural inclination toward conversion, the governance layer is the difference between a functional system and a non-functional one; for visitors already near alignment, a naive LLM's empathetic defaults are largely sufficient. All findings are conditional on LLM-to-LLM simulation. The PID controller has not been calibrated against real human unpredictability, and validating EO on live traffic is the critical next step.

</details>


### [255] [Distribird: Literature-Informed Prior Distribution Design for Bayesian Model Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11210)
*Patrik P. Süli,György Eigner,Roland Hollós*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Distribird is an agentic web application that automates the construction of informative priors for Bayesian calibration of process-based models by searching scientific literature, extracting and weighting parameter values by domain relevance, and fitting probability distributions via AIC model selection. When literature is unavailable, it falls back to uninformative priors, and it clearly reports evidence and confidence levels for each prior.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Building informative priors from scientific literature is currently slow and requires both domain and statistical expertise. Despite decades of methodological work, researchers almost always fall back on uniform priors because the process of constructing informative priors is labor-intensive.

Method: Distribird deploys a multi-agent pipeline that searches the literature, extracts and weights reported values by domain relevance, and fits a probability distribution via AIC model selection. It uses three open-weight models (Qwen3.6 27B, Gemma 4 31B, Mistral Small 4 119B) compared with a single-prompt LLM baseline. All language-model calls run locally, and a built-in validity layer declines to produce priors for out-of-scope requests.

Result: On prior quality the full pipeline matches the single-prompt LLM baseline. Every prior is traced to specific papers and values. The validity layer correctly declines out-of-scope requests, while the baseline returns confident but unfounded priors in 11 of 30 model–parameter cases. All LLM calls run locally, ensuring no parameter descriptions or modelling details are transmitted to third-party providers.

Conclusion: For scientific use, properties such as traceability to specific literature, built-in validity checks, and privacy (local LLM execution) matter more than marginal improvements in point-estimate accuracy. Distribird provides a trustworthy, automated approach to prior construction for physically interpretable model parameters.

Abstract: Bayesian calibration of process-based models requires a prior distribution for each model parameter. Despite decades of methodological work, researchers almost always fall back on uniform priors. The main reason is that building informative priors from scientific literature is slow and needs both domain and statistical expertise. We present \textbf{Distribird}, an agentic web application that automates this process. Given a parameter name, physical description, and domain context, Distribird deploys a multi-agent pipeline that searches the literature, extracts and weights reported values by domain relevance, and fits a probability distribution via AIC model selection. When no literature is available, the system falls back to sensible uninformative alternatives, and clearly reports both the evidence behind and the confidence level of every prior it produces. It is designed for the problems where the models have physically interpretable parameters, where domain knowledge exists in the published literature. We evaluate the tool on 24~parameters across 10 scientific domains comparing three open-weight models (Qwen3.6 27B, Gemma 4 31B, Mistral Small 4 119B) with a single-prompt LLM baseline. On prior quality the full pipeline \emph{matches} this baseline. Every prior is traced to the specific papers and values from which it was constructed; a built-in validity layer declines to produce priors for out-of-scope requests, whereas the single-prompt baseline returns confident but unfounded priors for them in 11 of 30~model--parameter cases; and every language-model call runs locally, so no parameter description or unpublished modelling detail is transmitted to a third-party LLM provider (only generated search terms reach the public literature databases). For scientific use, we argue these properties matter more than a marginal improvement in point-estimate accuracy.

</details>


### [256] [A Forced-Structure Reduction and Verifiable Bounds for Conway's 99-Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11211)
*Aalok Thakkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文报告了由自主AI研究代理对Conway 99-图问题（是否存在参数为srg(99,14,1,2)的强正则图）的系统性、可完全复现的攻击。主要贡献包括：证明循环图无法满足超过68.0%的约束；通过强制结构将问题简化为84个顶点上的12-正则图；建立并验证了预设自同构轨道存在性框架；以及获得最佳验证结果为69.43%的约束满足率，且14种不同方法均未超越该边界，表明该边界可能是鲁棒前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conway 99-图问题是图论中一个长期未解决的开放问题，询问是否存在参数为(99,14,1,2)的强正则图。该问题与有限几何、组合设计和代数图论密切相关。作者旨在通过自主AI研究代理进行系统性搜索，要么找到该图，要么通过约束满足边界证明其不存在。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 穷举证明在Z/99上的循环图最多满足4950个约束中的3366个（68.0%），即最多33个差分类（共49个），且对另一个阶为99的阿贝尔群有相同上界；(2) 强制结构简化：利用λ=1使每个邻域为完美匹配，μ=2使外部顶点与非匹配邻点对双射，将存在性问题简化为84个顶点上的12-正则图，并用CP-SAT编码，通过恢复唯一的srg(9,4,1,2)验证；(3) 验证的预设自同构轨道存在性框架（无不动点和单不动点作用），在srg(9,4,1,2)和Paley图srg(13,6,2,3)上检验；以及(4) 通过14种不同方法收敛到最佳验证工件69.43%，表明该边界鲁棒。

Result: 最佳验证工件达到69.43%的约束满足率（即4950个约束中满足3437个？原文未明确数字但给出百分比）。14种不同方法均未超越此边界，表明这是鲁棒前沿。任何低于4950的可证明边界都是不存在性证明，因此该结果与开放问题纠缠：若69.43%是最大可能满足率，则图不存在。此外，循环图和阿贝尔群情况的上界为68.0%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明Conway 99-图很可能不存在，但尚未完全证明。69.43%的约束满足前沿可能是该问题的极限，任何超越该边界的可证明上界都将证伪存在性。研究展示了自主AI代理在解决经典组合问题中的潜力，并提供了可复现的攻击框架。

Abstract: Conway's 99-graph problem asks whether a strongly regular graph with parameters $\mathrm{srg}(99,14,1,2)$ exists. We report a systematic, fully reproducible attack by an autonomous AI research agent, scored under the track's partial-credit metric. Our verifiable contributions are: (1) an exhaustive proof that no circulant graph on $\mathbb{Z}/99$ satisfies more than $3366/4950=68.0\%$ of the constraints ($33$ of $49$ difference-classes), with the same ceiling for the other abelian group of order $99$; (2) a forced-structure reduction: $λ=1$ makes each neighbourhood a perfect matching and $μ=2$ puts the outer vertices in bijection with non-matched neighbour-pairs, collapsing existence to a $12$-regular graph on $84$ vertices, encoded for CP-SAT and validated by recovering the unique $\mathrm{srg}(9,4,1,2)$; (3) a validated prescribed-automorphism orbit-existence framework (fixed-point-free and single-fixed-point actions, checked on $\mathrm{srg}(9,4,1,2)$ and the Paley graph $\mathrm{srg}(13,6,2,3)$), and (4) a best verified artifact at $69.43\%$, with evidence that this is a robust frontier (fourteen distinct methods, none exceeding it) entangled with the open question, since any provable bound below $4950$ is a non-existence proof.

</details>


### [257] [Detecting a Route Flip Is Easier Than Knowing Whether to Fix It: Causal Route-Mediated Damage in Quantized Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11212)
*Parvel Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究Top-k混合专家（MoE）路由的离散性导致KV缓存量化（4-bit）时，数值扰动（模拟量化通过受保护的BF16门读取）会改变专家选择，从而引发性能损伤。作者未提出新缓解方法，而是建立了因果分析框架、经验发现和检测极限结果。核心概念是路由介导分数（RMF）——量化损伤中由路由改变引起的比例。在OLMoE-1B-7B模型的4-bit KV缓存实验中，约三分之一损伤源于路由（RMF~0.31）。可部署的路由器边际（router margin）能检测到专家翻转（AUC 0.772），但无法区分有益与有害翻转。预注册实验跨三种架构验证，发现符号翻转税和符号不可分离性跨模型成立，而干净的参考补救收益受架构调节。实际int4 KV内核的结果与模拟剂量曲线兼容但统计效力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常假设KV缓存量化仅导致数值噪声，但本文指出Top-k MoE路由的离散性会放大这种噪声——量化扰动可能改变token的路由决策，导致不同的专家被激活。作者旨在量化这种路由介导的量化损伤比例，探索是否可以通过可观测的路由统计量来预测和修复这种损伤，并建立严格的因果归因框架。

Method: 1) 提出“路由介导分数（RMF）”作为量化损伤中由路由改变引起的比例；2) 设计四轮实验装置（four-run apparatus）来估计RMF；3) 采用token级归因分解损伤机制；4) 使用预注册假说和探针，跨三种架构（OLMoE等）进行验证；5) 分析多种路由器统计量（如边际、熵等）对翻转方向和损失符号的预测能力；6) 通过受控的同一检查点标志交换实验，分离门控归一化约定对损伤的影响；7) 比较模拟量化与真实int4 KV内核的结果。

Result: 1) OLMoE-1B-7B在4-bit KV量化下，RMF约0.31（即约1/3的量化损伤由路由改变介导）；2) 路由器边际（margin）可检测翻转发生（AUC 0.772），但无法区分有益翻转（减少损失）和有害翻转（增加损失）——在测试的局部、推理可观测的路由器统计量中，没有找到高于随机水平的翻转损失符号预测器；3) 符号翻转税（signed-flip tax）和符号不可分离性（sign-inseparability）跨模型成立；4) 干净参考（clean-reference）补救收益受架构调节；5) 门控归一化约定是损伤幅度的调节变量，而非路由可恢复性机制；6) 实际int4 KV内核的RMF与模拟量化曲线兼容，但统计效力不足（95% CI包含零）。

Conclusion: 量化对MoE模型的损伤部分来自路由决策的改变，且当前无法通过可观测的路由统计量区分有益/有害翻转，这构成了一个“效益检测屏障”（benefit-detection barrier），限制了仅依赖该特征家族的选择性修复。研究强调了路由介导损伤的重要性，并指出未来需要开发能够区分翻转符号的检测方法，或设计对量化更鲁棒的路由机制。

Abstract: Top-k Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routing is discontinuous, so a deployment-motivated numerical disturbance -- simulated 4-bit KV-cache quantization read by a protected BF16 gate -- pushes tokens across decision boundaries and flips which experts fire. This paper proposes no new mitigation; it supplies a causal apparatus, empirical findings, and a detection-limit result. A four-run apparatus prices the route-mediated fraction (RMF) of quantization damage, a token-level attribution decomposes it by mechanism, and pre-registered probes carry the findings across three architectures. On OLMoE-1B-7B at 4-bit KV (pilot), about a third of the damage is routing-mediated: RMF ~ 0.31 (discovery 0.31 [0.20, 0.41]; process-replicated mean 0.313 +/- 0.020; pre-registered re-execution 0.231). The deployable router margin detects that a flip occurred (AUC 0.772) but cannot tell a harmful flip from a helpful one (at chance): among the tested local, inference-observable router statistics we find no predictor of a flip's loss sign above chance -- an empirical benefit-detection barrier bounding selective repair restricted to this feature family. The signed-flip tax and sign-inseparability carry cross-model; the clean-reference remedy's payout is architecture-modulated; a controlled same-checkpoint flag-swap re-scopes the gate's normalization convention to a damage-magnitude moderator, not a route-recoverability mechanism. A real int4 KV kernel yields a fraction compatible with the fake-quant dose curve but underpowered (95% CI [-0.111, 0.394] includes zero) -- ruling out gross disagreement, not an independent replication. Hypotheses, thresholds, and evaluations were pre-registered before measurement, with misses reported; a pre-registered held-out read replicates the partition and the near-cancelling tax out of sample, while the strict impossibility exclusion narrowly misses.

</details>


### [258] [Poor Man's Agentic Modeling: Simulating Large LLM-Agent Societies on a Laptop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11215)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种方法，用少量参数的低成本代理模型替代大规模LLM模拟中的每个智能体，从而在笔记本电脑上运行任意规模的社会模拟。作者引入[交互阶×记忆]分类体系，将感知和记忆映射为有效理论，并预测代理误差随智能体数量N的变化趋势。在EconAgent等八个LLM模拟上验证，预测误差趋势逐单元成立；两个被反驳的预测（涉及强饱和响应及其曲率）也被理论无自由参数地定量匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM智能体社会模拟计算成本高昂，但所探究的问题通常是宏观层面的（相行为、典型事实、智能体数量的标度关系），而非单个智能体的认知细节。现有方法难以在低成本下保持宏观保真度，亟需一种可扩展的高效替代方案。

Method: 将统计物理的观察转化为方法：用从数百到数千次廉价查询中拟合出的低参数模型替换每个LLM智能体，然后在笔记本电脑上运行任意规模N的社会模拟。引入[交互阶×记忆]分类体系，将智能体的感知和记忆映射为有效理论，并预先预测代理误差随N的变化趋势。

Result: 在EconAgent及其他七个已命名的LLM模拟上验证，所有智能体决策均来自真实LLM（主要为DeepSeek）的少量美元成本诱发。预测的误差趋势逐单元格成立；两个被反驳的预测（均涉及强饱和响应并追溯至其曲率）被理论无自由参数地定量匹配。

Conclusion: 该代理建模方法能有效再现宏观模拟行为，且误差可预测；所提出的分类学提供了一种原则性方法，可在运行完整模拟前判定此类近似是否有效。

Abstract: Simulating societies of many large language model (LLM) agents is expensive, yet the questions asked of such simulations are usually macroscopic: phase behaviour, stylised facts, and scaling with the number of agents $N$, not the cognition of any single agent. We turn a statistical-physics observation into a method: replace each LLM agent by a low-parameter model fitted from a few hundred to a few thousand cheap queries, then run the society at any $N$ on a laptop. Whether this works is decided before the simulation runs, chiefly by what each agent perceives. We introduce an [interaction order x memory] taxonomy that maps perception and memory to an effective theory and a predicted $N$-trend of the surrogate error. We validate it on a faithful reimplementation of the LLM macroeconomy EconAgent and seven further named LLM simulations, with agent decisions cloned from genuine LLM elicitations (primarily DeepSeek) for a few dollars; the predicted error trends hold cell by cell, and the two refuted predictions, both on a strongly saturating response and traced to its curvature, are themselves matched quantitatively by the theory with no free parameters.

</details>


### [259] [AutoWorldModel-Bench: A State-Centric Benchmark for Automated World-Model Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11216)
*Marjan Moodi,Xuankang Zhu,Fernando De Mesentier Silva,Harold Chaput,Mohammad Reza Taesiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoWorldModel-Bench，一个闭环基准测试，用于评估前沿编码智能体在固定计算预算下自主改进世界模型的能力。该基准涵盖8个游戏环境，使用统一的结构化状态表示，将动态建模与感知分离。实验表明，Codex-5.4和Claude Opus 4.6在64次会话中63次改进了初始模型，其中91%的获胜编辑是研究型修改而非超参数调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界建模是一个尚未成熟的领域，架构、训练目标和状态表示之间存在复杂交互，没有单一方法能在所有环境中占主导地位。这使其成为评估AI编码智能体作为自主研究者的理想测试平台——改进方向不是预先指定的，这与当前以工程规范任务为主的智能体基准不同。

Method: 设计了一个闭环基准AutoWorldModel-Bench，前沿编码智能体在固定计算预算下自主改进提供的世界模型初始版本。基准涵盖8个游戏环境，采用统一的结构化状态表示——从每个游戏中提取真实实体状态，通过共享张量格式输入——从而将动态建模与感知分离，实现每次运行分钟级的迭代。

Result: 在64次会话中，Codex-5.4和Claude Opus 4.6在63次中改进了初始模型；91%的会话中获胜编辑是非平凡的研究型修改——新的目标函数、表示、展开过程或架构变更——而非超参数调整。

Conclusion: AutoWorldModel-Bench提供了一个评估前沿编码智能体在开放式研究而非工程规范任务上的能力的场景。

Abstract: World modeling is an unsettled field: architectures, training objectives, and state representations interact in complex ways, and no single recipe dominates across environments. This makes it an ideal testbed for AI coding agents acting as autonomous researchers--a setting in which the improvement direction is not specified in advance, unlike the engineering-to-spec tasks that dominate current agent benchmarks. We introduce AutoWorldModel-Bench, a closed-loop benchmark in which frontier coding agents autonomously improve a provided world-model starter under a fixed compute budget. The benchmark spans eight game environments under a unified structured-state representation--ground-truth entity state extracted from each game and consumed through a shared tensor format--which isolates dynamics modeling from perception and enables minutes-per-run iteration. Across 64 sessions, Codex-5.4 and Claude Opus 4.6 improve their starter on 63; in 91% of sessions the winning edit is a non-trivial research-style modification--a new objective, representation, rollout procedure, or architectural change--rather than a hyperparameter tweak. Our benchmark offers a setting in which frontier coding agents can be evaluated on open-ended research rather than engineering-to-spec problems.

</details>


### [260] [MaSRead: Content-Addressed Reading of Replicated Latent Stores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11218)
*Carlos Baquero,Luís Brito,João Resende*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MaSRead 方法，使复制的潜在状态存储（CRDT 合并的键值缓存片段）能够被后续查询按内容选择性地读取。该方法通过基于片段单词的不透明键标签路由、硬注意力掩码解码以及词汇连通性图游走实现多跳查询，在多种存储结构（链、管道、对称、枢纽、自然语言）下有效，且能跨模型家族迁移。解码开销取决于片段长度而非总存储大小，但词汇路由可能遗漏不连通的证据，答案组合受限于冻结的阅读器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立智能体在潜在空间中推理时，以键值缓存片段而非文本形式共享计算状态。这些片段通过无冲突复制数据类型（CRDT）合并成存储，能在任意投递顺序或重复下收敛。然而，后续查询（编码时未知）无法可靠读取合并后的缓存：共存的片段相互干扰，因此共存不等于可寻址。需要解决“根据内容读取”的问题。

Method: MaSRead 方法通过以下步骤实现按内容读取：（1）路由：利用从片段词汇派生的不透明键标签集合，路由到相关片段；（2）解码：对每个选中的片段在硬注意力掩码下解码，掩盖其他片段；（3）对于多跳查询，基于词汇连通性进行图游走，到达所需片段。该方法在链式、管道、对称、枢纽和自然语言存储等多种结构上进行了评估。

Result: MaSRead 能在各种存储结构中恢复访问过的片段并保持隔离，即使无关片段不断累积仍保持有效，并能迁移到另一个模型家族（如从 Llama 到 Mistral）。路由后，物化解码的开销取决于片段长度而非总存储大小；端到端工作仍包含与存储相关的路由和每个访问片段的一次读取。词汇路由可能遗漏不连通的证据，答案组合受限于冻结的阅读器。

Conclusion: 当所需片段通过内容与查询连接时，复制的潜在存储可以实现选择性可读。但该方法存在明确限制：词汇路由无法发现不连通的证据，且答案组合能力受限于预训练的阅读器。总体而言，MaSRead 提供了一种在复制潜在存储中进行内容寻址的有效机制。

Abstract: Independent agents that reason in latent space can share computed state as key-value cache fragments rather than text. Merged by a conflict-free replicated data type, these fragments form a store that converges under any delivery order or duplication. Yet a later query, unknown at encode time, cannot reliably read the merged cache: colocated fragments interfere, so colocation is not addressability. MaSRead addresses the read to content. It routes through opaque keyed tag sets derived from fragment words and decodes each selected fragment under a hard attention mask that hides the rest. Under lexical connectivity, a graph walk reaches the fragments required by a multi-hop query. Across chain, pipeline, symmetric, hub, and natural-language stores, MaSRead recovers visited fragments in isolation, remains effective as unrelated fragments accumulate, and transfers to another model family. After routing, materialized decoding depends on fragment length rather than total store size; end-to-end work still includes store-dependent routing and one read per visited fragment. The limits are explicit: lexical routing can miss disconnected evidence, and answer composition remains bounded by the frozen reader. Thus a replicated latent store becomes selectively readable for later queries when the needed fragments connect to the query through content.

</details>


### [261] [From Monolithic to Modular: Segment-level Automatic Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11219)
*Nikita Kulin,Viktor Zhuravlev,Artur Khairullin,Sergey Muravyov,Ilya Makarov,Daniil Sukhorukov,Ekaterina Averkova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SAPO，一种基于段级（segment-level）的自动提示优化方法，将提示分解为角色、上下文、任务和输出格式四个片段，利用 top-5/bottom-5 样例进行针对性改进，在多个基准上超越零样本和现有 APO 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 APO 方法对整个提示进行整体重写，容易在提升某个行为的同时损害其他行为。需要一种更精细的优化方式，能定位并改善提示中具体片段的问题，同时保持其他片段的有效性。

Method: SAPO 将提示分解为角色、上下文、任务和输出格式四个段（segment），通过一个 LLM 配合静态元提示和结构化输出来完成分割、弱点分析和候选生成。采用训练/验证协议，两阶段生成：(1) 段级诊断与推荐提取；(2) 基于弱/强段信号的候选合成。

Result: 在 SQuADv2、TweetEval、XSUM、CommonGen 和 GSM8K 上使用 GPT-3.5-Turbo 和 GPT-4o-mini 进行评估，SAPO 的平均得分优于 Zero-shot 以及 APE、OPRO、EvoPrompt、GEPA 和 StraGO 等强 APO 基线。

Conclusion: 段级自动提示优化能够更精确地定位和修复提示中的薄弱环节，避免整体重写带来的副作用，从而获得更优的整体性能。

Abstract: Automatic Prompt Optimization (APO) often rewrites prompts monolithically, which can improve one behavior while degrading others. We present SAPO, a segment-level APO method that decomposes prompts into role, context, tasks, and output format, then applies targeted improvements based on top-5 and bottom-5 examples. The optimization loop uses one LLM with static meta-prompts and structured outputs for segmentation, weakness analysis, and candidate generation. We describe a train/validation protocol and a two-stage generation process: (1) segment-level diagnosis and recommendation extraction, (2) candidate synthesis constrained by weak/strong segment signals. Using the evaluation setup across SQuADv2, TweetEval, XSUM, CommonGen, and GSM8K on GPT-3.5-Turbo and GPT-4o-mini, SAPO achieves the best average score against Zero-shot and strong APO baselines including APE, OPRO, EvoPrompt, GEPA, and StraGO.

</details>


### [262] [LLMs in Process Diagram Engineering: From Optimal PFDs to Validated P&IDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11220)
*Timur Zakarin,Sergei Voitov,Sergei Shumilin,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了P&ID Pilot，一个端到端的人工智能管道，用于将工艺流程图(PFD)自动转换为管道和仪表图(P&ID)。第一阶段使用遗传算法(GA)和大语言模型(LLM)的混合方法生成最优PFD拓扑，第二阶段使用基于LLM的代理将PFD转换为经验证的P&ID，实现了100%的执行成功率，并遵守领域特定规则。该管道减少了手动工程工作量，为工艺设计自动化提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前PFD创建及其到P&ID的转换主要靠手动完成，耗时且劳动密集。应用人工智能可以自动化流程、节省时间、探索多种拓扑选项并减少人工劳动，从而带来财务收益。

Method: 提出了一个端到端AI管道，包括两个阶段：第一阶段通过比较四种方法，采用遗传算法(GA)和大语言模型(LLM)的混合方法生成最优的PFD拓扑；第二阶段使用基于LLM的代理，通过受限的工程软件开发工具包生成可执行的修改，将PFD转换为源接地P&ID。

Result: 混合方法(GA+LLM)在所有方法中实现了最低的损失值，满足出口流量参数且无工程规则违规。第二阶段的LLM代理实现了100%的执行成功率，并保持了领域特定规则和参考图结构的合规性。

Conclusion: 统一管道将GA/LLM驱动的合成与基于LLM的转换代理相结合，为端到端工艺设计自动化提供了可行路径，生成了经过验证、可部署的输出，并大幅减少了手动工程工作量。
